Sani, Umar da sadiya..Haka suka taso cikin kulawa da jin dadi da kaunar yan uwansu, Alh musa wada ya sasu a makarantar Boko da islamiya Dan yanada Hali daidai gwargwado...inda Sadiya nagama secondary school akai mata aure kasancewar ta diya Mace.... Bayan auren ne Alhj da matarsa bilkisu sukai acccident suka Rasu Allah yaji Kansu... *Amin*...Yan Uwa anyi kuka Sosai sbd ba bakaramin rasa tsohon kirki akaiba ....yaran ma ba a magana Dan sunci Kuka Sosai........

Dan tasan abun nan akwai ​ *ASIRI* a ciki​ bata fadawa kowaba se Aminiyarta *Samira* dan Ko anty larai batasan meke faruba ba...samira itama ta dage da rokawa kawarta Akan Allah ya kawo karshen abunnan, 
*Samira* tai tagumi Tace ikon Allah, Ni kingani Banzauna da kishiyata ba uwar gidana Amma nasan bazatai mun Hakaba......

Haka rayuwa ta ci gaban musu, haj *biba* ta riga ta gama asirce Alhj umar, dan kwata kwata yau shekara daya, yadena picking call dinta, ballema ya zao ta ganshi, 
tun tana damuwa amma yanzu har ta dena ya bi mata jiki, dan kullun seta tino da *maganar iyayanta ​nabin mijinta sau da kafa​* taci gaba da addua dan tana samun kwanciyar hankali, matsalan ta daya shine, abincin ta ya kare tamai magana amma kamar ba da mutun take magana ba, Abinci kam sedai taje wajen samira, tanaci dan 
*Nafeesa* dama already anty samira ta amsheta, dan Alhaj gaba daya ya manta yanada mata balle wata kuma yarinya ​Feenah​ dan tun randa Alhj ya nuna ma feenah so a gaban Haj beebah, taje tai asiri, akan ya manta da ita....
Tayi nasara kam dan ya manta dasu gaba daya.....maimuna kuma Abun yafi karfinta se *INNALILLAHI WA INNAILAIHI RAJIUN* take yawan fada....

Love you All...Readers da writers Allah yai mana albarka💋

8⃣2⃣
*BANGAREN* gidan🏩 Mal Sani Kuma yayan Alhaj umar, Abun ya matukar bashi mamaki dan Rabonshi da dan uwansa harya Manta, Dan  yanzu malan sani karayan arziki ta sameshi beda kudi kaman Da yanzu dillalancin da yakeyi Sai aslow, Dan ba wani kudi yake samuba, So Jummai Yau mafisan yau daban na gobe daban gashi badama tace ya saketa, Ko takai kara ba yarda zasuiba dan dama wan babanta ya riga ya mata kashedi karta kuskura ta kawo kara.........''Hmm mata kenan Idan zamuso mutun mu Soshi dan Allah bawai abun hannunshiba ko kyau, dan Komai Allah na chanzawa alokacin da yaso''....haka nan ta hakura dan ba yanda za tai, Yau asamu gobe kuma baza'a samuba, Shi kuma dan tilon dansu daya *Adam* secondary school yake be  kawo karfi ba tukunna ko ya samu, na makarantarshi yake tanadawa.....rayuwa kenan...
A kasar senegal kumA Baba Alhaji ya kara tsufa amma kullun da tinanin yarsa yake mafarkai barkatai yakeyi akanta amma koda ya fadawa maman maimuna sai tace alhaji ba komai, tunda mukaji shuru nasan lafia lau, da akwai wani abu ko bata kirabuba zata kira Larai, kuma zata fada mana yace hakane, nan dai suka ci gaba da hiransu irin na tsofaffin Masoya....gwanin sha'awa..true love never end😘.....
nan na barsu bace bari na nayo kaduna na leka  dakin *Beebah*😁 me alhaji lolz Ina daga labule naga beebah tasha wani shadda pink ta harda kafa tana latsa waya ta sake wani murmushi naga tayi dialing din numbern aminiyarta haj Rabi, tace hello 
ta amsa da madan Ranki ya dade, 
suka gaisa tace ya labari kwana biyu, 
nan dai tace wlhi kam, Kinsan maganinan nan ba karamin aiki yamun ba dan Alhaji ya manta da wata mata, 
tace very good ai nasan baki wasa da abunda kikeso tace yeah ashe kingane.... Sukai sallama,,...haka rayuwa taci gaba dai dan *maimuna* gaba daya ta rame ta kode abunka da hips da boobs duk tai wani iri sbd ba abinci mekyau, Bayan wata *4* kenan aka aiko mata da rasuwar mamanta,.. *kulli nafsin za'akatul maut*😭😭.....
hankalinta ya tashi Sosai tai kuka kamar ranta zai fita, Tazo side din haj biba tace alhj nanan tace eh, yananan...
haj biba ta juya ta kyalkyale da dariyan mugunta, Ta shiga ko kallon arziki be mataba, ta sanar dashi mamantane ta Rasu, yace Allah yajikanta, dan ko alamar damuwa beyiba, 
Yace idan taga dama taje danshi ba inda zaije yanada abunyi sosai....

8⃣3⃣
*SUBHANALLAH.......*
maimuna bata damuba dan macece me hakuri, sosai dan tasan bayin kanshi bane harda *asiri* a ciki, ta dawo daki jiki a sanyaye ta kira samira ta fada mata rasuwar mamanta, ta tautsaya mata, ta bata kudi sosai, sannan ta shugo gidanta ta kira Anty Larai ta sanar da ita rasuwar, 
Washe gari nayi sega *anty Larai* nan gidan dan ta zone su tafi senegal din Tare,...da isowarta ta kalla👀😳😳 maimuna, ta jijjigata tace *Maimuna* lafiyanki kuwa, meka faruwa dakene, ba abun da maimuna takeyi inba kuka ba, kasancewar 
*Da yake Samira* nanan Ne Tace Anty Larai is a long story ki bari kuje ku dawo zan sanar dake duk abun da ke faruwa, Larai ta juya kallon ​ *Samira*​ tace Waye ke Kuma........ 

*_Thanks for your time sisters...Nagode Sosai_*💞💞💞💞💞

_*SAMIRA* tace agurguje ni sunana Samira iyayena suna garin Katsina, a dalilin aurene Mijina *KaMal* ya kawoni kaduna inda muka zama makota da Maimuna, Alhaj Kamal dan siyasane sosai yanada mata daya amma Ta rasu, Yaran Alhj Kamal hudu, babban danshi, sunansa Naseer sai usman, sai autan dakinsu Zainab, yanzu haka ni yarona Dayane zayyad_,  
_Naseer yana Abuja yana karatu don be cika zama anan ba, usman ma boarding school yake be zama sai Autan dakin maringanya Hindatu zainab, Ita munanan tare da ita se yarona kwara daya zayyad......kinji atakaice kenan_, Anty larai kuzo ku tafi dan lokaci na kurewa....Tace ok nagode sosai idan ko nadawo zan nemeki dan dole insan abun dake Damun yata..........nan sukai sallama maimuna se kuka takeyi ga bakin cikin Alhj ga Rashin mahaifiyarta......Sun isa sunyi gaisuwa kwana bakwai sukai agarin kowa dayaga maimuna seya tausaya mata dan tinanainsu rasuwar mahaifiyarta ce yasata haka, nan ko ba wanda yasani dan ko anty larai bazatace ga abun dake damuntaba..... Ana gobe zasu tafi kamar bazasu rabu da mahaifinta ba yace ina *Jikata feenah* duk angama gaisuwan nan ban gantaba tace tana wajen abokiyar zamana sbd school sai yasa banzo da itaba yace ok....ya Alhaji kuma, nan gabanta ya fasi sosai tace yananan zuwa aikine sukai mai yawa da tare zamuzo, yace ok, adai ci gaba damai biyayya ga Larai nan ki riketa tamkar ita ta haifeki dan kinga yanzu baki da uwa nima na kusan mutuwa...anan aka fara sabon koke koke sukai ma'ishinsu sannan suka hakura,.......washe gari suka kama hanya Sai gida nigeria✈..............

8⃣4⃣

A gidan Alhaj Umar suka sauka, ita da haj larai nan Samira najin alamun sundawo ta shirya nafeesa suka zo, bayan sun gaisa ta kara yi musu ya karin Hakuri da gajiyan Hanya, Suka amsa da lafia lau sun gode,
 naafeeh se jin dadi take mamanta ta dawo kasancewar batafi *4* years ba batasan meye mutuwaba sai yasa bata damu sosai ba abunka da yaro, sun huta sunci abinci anty larai ta umurci samira data Fada mata abun dake faruwa, nan samira ta kwashe labarin komai *FROM A TO Z*, harda na Rashin abinci da yama manta feenah yarshice.. *DALILIN* da yasa samira ta dauka Nafisa kenan duk yawancin Nauyinta samira ta dauka, 
Anty Larai ta numfasa ta Wanka ma maimuna Mari gudu biyu, 
Tace *WHAT*😳... Maimuna baki da hankaline ko sun rufe miki bakine da bazaki zo ki sanar dani komai ba, Wannan zurfin cikin nan naki wlhi ki dena ba hali bane, da banzoba kuma ban tarar da yar albarkan nan *samiraba* da shikkenan se bakin ciki ya kasheki ai mu zasui wa asara, Nai dai Samira tace anty larai ayi hakuri, tunda Yanzu kinsan komai, se mu ci gaba da addua mu neman mata mafita, Ita ko maimuna se zabga kokanta takeyi,anty larai tace dole mu dage da addua, dan bazamu kauce hanyaba, 
samira tace sosai, *Addua shine takobin mumine*, nan dai suketa jajanta wa junansu taya zasu bullo wa wannan abun, har yamma tayi, Sukai sallama anty larai tace zandawo kwana biyu inji ya ake Aci, ya chanza ko be chanzaba, anty larai ta tafi itama Samira ta koma gidanta dan Yamma yayi......

 *BAYAN KWANA BIYU* Anty larai ta dawo dan jin ya chanza ko a a, maimuna tace dai gskiya bezoba tunda ta dawo se matarshi biba itama nasan gulma tazoyi bawai gaisuwan tsakani da Allah bane, Anty Larai ta fusata tace bani Number me gidan naki, nan take ta bata ta kirashi yace waye Tace nice *haj Larai*, jikinshi na bari ya gaisheta, tace ina side din Maimuna kazo yanzunnan ina Son Ganinka, *Alhj Umar* jiki na rawa yana dakin *beebah*  yace bari yaje ya dawo duk a tsorace yake saboda tsoron Anty Larai yake sosai, Gaban Beebah ya fadi tace ina kuma zaka, be amsataba ya fito da sauyi, taga yayi sasan maimuna seda ta kusan fitsari a wando dan ta mugun tsorata, tace meke shirin faruwa danine, tana ganin ya shige part din maimuna ta koma daki da sauri ta dauka waya ta kira kawarta rabi, 
Haj rabi tace hello
Beebah  fasa kuka tace na shiga uku, tace menenen tace Alhj umar ne ya tafi site din maimuna, itama seda ta tsorata tace kawata sai kinyi da gaske dan asirinmu na gab da tunuwa....sukai Sallama Beebah ta kasa zama se yawo takeyi a tsakar Daki.

8⃣5⃣

Alh Umar na shiga yai sallama, suka Amsa, umar duk a gigice sbd tsoro da fargaban abun da za ace yayi, dakyar ya sama waje  ya zauna duk kunya ta isheshi har wani kumatu yayi na musamman, ita ko *beloved*😘 Se satan kallonshi takeyi taji wani farin ciki sosai a tattare da ita, hajiya larai tace *Umar* meke faruwane, Na tambaya maimunah bata ce mun komai ba shine nakiraka na tambayeka, meke faruwane, Ko dai baka sontane a raba auran, Alhaj umar ya zaro ido😳 
yace i'm sorry wlhi ba haka bane bansan meke daukemun  hankaliba amma wlhi inason matata, Nan dai tamai nasiha, har take cemai maman maimuna ta rasu ko kazo,  kaman zaiyi magana kuma se ya manta, yace Allah yaji kanta, Abun ya daure musu kai matuka, amma to ya zasui kowa ya gani yasan wannan abun asirine, tace ya matarka nan dai ya washe baki tananan lafia, tace to kadai dinga adalci a tsakaninsu, Kuma maimuna bata da abinci kasai mata inba hakaba za'a raba auran dole....
Yace ok Bakomai insha Allah zanyi iya kokarina, 
sukai sallama ya tashi ya fita, koda ya fita ba dakin beebah ya je ba *Bank* yaje ya ciro kudi yaje yai wa matarshi shoping da nafeesah, harda kayan sawa, kafin ya dawo anty larai tai gida, Yana shugowa beebah se leke ta wando take taga Alhaj da kaya tanasha natane kuma se taga ya wuce side din maimuna Nan ta kwalla wani irin ihu, Tana kuka tace yau na shiga Tara, yanzu shikkenan sunyi nasara akaina, *dama Tun tafiyansu senegal haj beebah ta rabu da zuwa wajen bokanta*, dole ta sake kiran kawarta ta sanar mata da komai, Tace toh bana fada mikiba, Idan ana aiki da boka dole se ka dinga xuwa akai akai inba hakaba aikinka ba wuyuwa zaiyiba, 
*ALLAH YA TSAREMU* 
yana shiga beloved taga kaya se Hamdala takeyi a xuciyanta, bakuga murna ba wajen beloved, Tace baban feenah wannan duk nawane yace Eh..Ta godema  ALLAH sosai, Alhj umar na fita ta kira samira a waya ba'a jimaba se gata tazo, Nan ta nuna mata kayan da alhaj ya siyo mata Samira ta rungumeta tace *Mune da Nasara Insha Allah....mai hakuri mawadacine yanzu muci gaba da addua sosai....Allah Yana Tare Damu*😀,
 Ba laifi yanzu Alhaji yana Zuwa side dinta akai akai kuma yana bata kulawa amma ba kamar lokacin suna *AMARYA DA ANGO BA*😜....dan Akwai sihirin a tare dashi kadan.

8⃣6⃣

Haka suka ci gaba da rayuwa kusan *5 MONTHS* ba laifi alhaj na kokarin yin Adalci a tsakaninsu ba wani damuwa sosai,

 Bangaren Hajiya beebah kuma hankalinta a tashe yake Sosai sbd tun da ta shugo shuru takeji da ciki ko barin wata bata tabayiba....
hankalinta ya tashi sosai taga Alhaji umar na kwana a dakin maimuna shine take jin tsoron Kar Maimuna ta sama ciki su cinye gado😜.....dole ta sake nemar kawarta tace kawata ko zamu chanza wajen bokane, yanzu fa 5months tun aikin da nace yai mun yaqiyi kuma alhaj na shiga dakinta fa kar asama ciki nake tsoro, tace gskiya nima abun da nake guge miki kenan kar ki tashi ba ko sisi, indai kin shirya ko yanzune sekizo mu tafi dan asan abunyi, kar dare yai mana dan tana samun ciki shikkenan Mun kade,  tace bari kedai ganinan zuwa yanzu.....mu hadu a bakin hanya kawai dan sauri nakeyi ba sai na shigo ba, tace Ok suna haduwa suka kama hanya se wani cikin daji.........
suna ta tafiya duk sun gaji kafansu yai futufutu rabi tace nifa haji nagaji 
tace kiyi hakuri daga wannan shikkenan me kankat za ai tace hmm kawata bakiyin sanya a abun da kikeso tace yaushe ai kema kinsani, 
suna tafe suna hiransu irin na mugayen matan nan har suka Isa, Sukaji ko ina anata dariya amma basuga kowa ba, sukaji wata murya nacewa Ku tsaya a inda kuke kar ku kuskura ko motsa, sukai chak, Bayan minti biyar sukaji ance make tafe daku, Beebah tace abun dake tafe damu shine, Inaso A raba mijina da matarshi da yarshi yama manta dasu....
 *INNALILLAHI*, sukaji an fashe da dariya akace ba aiki me wuya Bane, amma kinzo da kudi Ko, tace eh akwai, sai sukaji shuru Nan dai basuga kowa ba aka jefo musu wani laya da da magani da Da wani kwalli, Yace laya kisa a kasan fillon Maigida, Magani kuma ki barbada mai a abinci, 
yanayin bacci kisa kwallin kizo ku hada ido da matarshi nan...nan dai sukai mai godiya yace keeee ba a godiya anan ku ajiye kudin ku ruga da gudu....sukai yanda Yace, ahanyane haj rabi tace kai da Wahala bazan sakeba wallahi, Har se musanyan magana suke suka daiyi fada, amma still tare suka dawo suna dawowa Rabi tace kar ki Sake kirana, 
Gskiya bazan iya ba, niba da yiba ni da samuna zunubi wallahi Na Tuba.... 
Kowa ya hakama hanyarshi, Tunda nake bantaba jin wuya nayau Ba....
*ALLAH YA YAFEMUN* inji hajiya rabi...ita dai beebah tace munafuka to mu rabu din akwai wasu, Dan mun rabu sai Me...Nan Ko sukai *BARAM BARAM*🙅🏻, Ba Hajiya Rabee ba Hajiya beebah..sun Bata.........

8⃣7⃣
Alhaji Yana dawowa Around *4pM* yai musu siyayya na Kayan kwalama, Yana shugowa Ya fara shiga site din Maimuna, habawa beebah najin motsin alamun ya dawo taje ta hadamai abinci tasamai maganin, ta tafi daki tasa mai laya a akasan pillow sbd itace da girki, 
Umar gabanshi na fadi  yana bata ya fito ya tafi side din *beebah*, tana ji ya shugo tazo tamai sannu da xuwa, tace naga ka gaji ga ruwan wanka nan yaje yyi, 
yana fitowa ya sama abinci yaci yace yagode..
bari nadan kwanta, Tace ok..Dadi fal aranta tasan dole yai bacci, 
Yana bacci tai maza tasa kwallin da bokanta ya bata ta taho tai ma maimuna knocking tana budewa, Suna hada Ido kafin ma tai magana taji wani Irin abu ya shigeta har kwakwalwanta, Nan take ta Kwalla Wani *IHU me Karfi*, 
*Allah sarki, ihun da tai har yasa ta zama Kurma, (dalilin Da maganin da boka ya ba beebah kenan dan tazama kurma)*
 beebah tai Sauri ta koma daki, Jin ihuntane samira ta shugo da sauri, tace maimuna menene, Maimuna ba um ba um um dan Ba wanda yasan Ta zama Kurma, Alhaj na jin ihunta ya tashi a fusace, ya nufo dakin maimuna, yace iskancin banza da wufi ina baccina zaki tasheni to na sakeki, ki fitanmun a gida bansan Ganinki, yai wucewarsa, 
maimuna me zatai in banda kuka, samira ta tsaya sokoko a tsaye tace wannan abun ba nawana bane, 
waya ta dakko Ta kira anty larai ta sanar da ita... *Ranta a bace tace sakamakon kenan*, 
ganinan zuwa, 
Tana zuwa tace ai shikkenan tunda ya saketa, bari na kira wanshi Mal Sani, yazo ya dauka nafeesa dan Ba inda zamu tafi da ita, ba da ita Maimuna tazoba  a gidan aka haifeta, Samira tace anty larai dan Allah abarmun ita 
anty larai tace kul. Ba ruwanki, 
MAL sani kuwa yana zuwa anty larai ta sanar dashi Komai halin da dan uwanshi yake ciki, yace wallahi nasani nima yaushe rabona dashi,
Tace to ga feenah nan, abu dai beyi dadiba, feenah na kuka, dakyar aka rabata da mamanta, *MAIMUNA KO* batace komai ba dan ba wanda yasan ta zama kurma sun aza tsaban tashin hankaline ya Janata magana, nan kowa ya kama gabansa 
*mal sani* ya tafi da nafeesa feenah,
*anty larai* tazo aka kwashe kayan Maimuna 
*samira* ma tai bakin ciki sosai amma ba yanda ta iya...tace zata dinga zuwa gidan akai akai tana dubata.......aka gama budirin nan ba motsin Beebah bare malan uwar....Dan shi besan ma ya saketa ba ba....kubiyoni

8⃣9⃣

Ba shiri ta nema wayarta Ta kira Samira
Samira nagani taji dadi ta dauka 
"Hello Anty larai, ina wuni
"Larai ta fashe da kuka tace shikkenan Samira sun nakasa mun yarinya, 
"samira tace Anty Larai me ya faru, tace sun kurmantar da Ita, Samira itama ta fashe da kuka ba dama tace abata maimuna su gaisa, tace innalillahi wannan ba magana ta waya bace zanzo very soon 
Anty larai tace ok
Samira tace yanzu ya kamata a dage da addua ne  har Allah ya sa ta warke, nan dai sukai sallama, Samira hankalinta a tashe tanata kuka, Tai Alwala tai Sallah ta roka Allah ya kara kareta daga sharrin Muguwar Kishiya, Sannan ta rokawa aminiyarta, waraka, Ta zauna jum, Zainab da zayyad sukace mumy meya faru tace Bakomai, Ku dakko qur'ani mu karanta Tare, nan suka dauko... *ALLAHU AKBAR*

*Haj beebah* kuwa tunda Alhaji ya sake matarshi wani dadi yabi ya isheta, Tanaji da alhaj kamar me, a ranar da suka bar gidan Beebah har party ta hada, Kawaye kowa se murna suke mata akan ta zama *hatsabibiya zama da kishiya sai Dole* alhaji kuwa Kwata kwata hankalinshi ya juye baya iya tina komai da kowa se shu'umar matanshi, Yanzu hankalinta kwance abun ta takeso ta Samu, washe gari kuwa ta daga waya ta kira kawarta hajiya Rabi tace kawas, Ya banganki a Partyn dana hadaba wanda nace idan nai nasara na kora kishiya zan hada,, 
"_rabi ta numfasa tace Tir da halinki, Wato seda kika aikata ko, nidai wallahi na tuba tun jiya da muka dawo, kuma ina rokan Allah yasa ki gane kema ki tuba, in zaki lura se wahala mukesha, muna yawo a rana😂 dan rainin wayau ma harda gudu Mukai, Allah ya sakawa kishiyarki, Nidai Allah ya yafemun, Kuma in kinada zuciya kar ki sake kiran wayana, dan zan chanza layi ne dan karma ki Sake nemana_, 
*Beebah* tace karyan banza, muje dake jiyajiyan nan yanzu kice kin tuba...
"Rabi tace *IKON ALLAH kenan*, Ni kinga sai anjuma bazan tsaya ina bata lokacina ba akanki, ta kashe waya *din din din*.....
*Bebah* ta kalla wayan tace, to kin ma kanki, da yanzun daulan Alhaji dake za aci, taja tsaki *mtswwww*,

9⃣0⃣
➡ Haka rayuwa taci gaba a kowani bangare...
Akwana atashi ba wuya, Yau take  ranar *SATURDAY* ranar da ko wani dan Aji shida yake farin ciki, Don Haka feenah ta tashi da wuri  ta shirya tai wa baba Sani da Umma sallama,  Mal Sani Ya ciro naira 15 ya bata yace tayi Hakuri Beda Kudi, ya kara da cewa Allah ya bada sa'a ta amsa tace amin, ta kama hanyanta ta tafi School, ta isa kenan Taga yara kowa na murna, daga masu basket a hannu se wanda aka ba kudin kashewa masu yawa dan Zasu dan Jima kafin Su dawo gida, nan take ta nema farin cikinta ta rasa; Ta sama waje ta zauna se rusa kuka takeyi, yara sai wucewa sukeyi suna kallonta, Kowa yazo yana tambayanta meya faru setai banza dashi taita kuka, Har Wani dan Saurayi (Corper) da bazai wuce 24yrs ba Yazo wucewa ya ganta Tana ta kuka, yaje wajenta Yace KEE, why Ar you crying, baki da lafia ne, Jin muryan namiji yasa ta dago fuskanta, ta Kasa magana Ya Daka mata tsawa badake nakeyiba, What's Wrong With You, ta kara kallonshi taga ashe guy din ya hadu, sai ta sake baki tana Kallonshi, Yaja tsaki yace mtswww Ji yarinya kamar aljana ana tambayanta amma Ta sake baki tana kallon mutun, Ya kara
Daka mata tsawan da yafi na farkon Yace *KEEEEEEE bazaki maganaba*, Ta rude ta fara magana a _shagwabe_ tace ba Dad dina bane ya sake mamana, aka kawoni wani gida yace To It's Ok ba abun da nakeson jiba kenan Yanzu Meye Ya saki kuka kuna gab da shiga exams, Tace Abun da ya sani kuka Shine YUNWA NAKEJI...
yace simple Ya Ciro 200 naira ya bata yace kije kici abinci,.........

_Hmmm readers nasan zasuso kuji waye Wannan Saurarayin Ku biyoni.......Love You All....._ 💋💋💋

Yana Mika Mata Ta Amsa taji dadi Sosai Shiko ya kama gabanshi Ko kallon baya beyiba, amma aranshi Yace Tunda yake be taba ganin yarinya kyakkyawaba kamar Ita...Yace Oh rayuwa kenan Wai yanzu duk kyan yarinyar nan ba a hannun iyayanta takeba, ya tafi dai yanata zancen Zuci.... Ita ko ta nufa exams Hall ta zauna aka raba masu answer sheet, data tina tanada 200 sai dadi ya isheta tai ta murna tana Dariya,  ana gama exams, yara kowa yaje yaci abun da yakeso itama kaman zata kashe kudin sai ta tina da dan uwanta Adam seta fasa kashewa tace Yaya Adam kai zanba kudin Nan..... *ANGAMA EXAMS* yara sunyi bankwana da School anata kukan missing😜 kowa ya kama hanyar gidansu, itako sauri sauri take tazo gida taba yaya Adam labari.

9⃣1⃣

Nafeesa na Isa gida ta tarar da umma A daki ta gaisheta ta amsa a wulaqance, Tace ya naga kin dade har yamma tace Wallahi Mun tsaya an dan mana Nasihace, Kafin result din mu ya fito ance mu dinga taya iyayanmu aiki da kyautata musu, Umma tace Wayyo gashi  uwartaki ba hannunta kike ba, nafeesa najin haka ta fashe da kuka umma ko kallonta batai ba, chan dai taga kukan nata bana kare bane tace rufemun baki shegiya, yarinya kamar Mayya kin iya zaro zance rumi rumi as an mini magana kisa kuka, Itadai nafeesa tayi kukanta sosai sannan ta kama bakinta tai shuru, kafin magrib se ga Baba sani ya dawo, tana ganinsa tace oyoyo baba, ka dawo lafia ya ce lafia lau yar Baba, ya jarabawan Tace Alhamdulillah, baba dan Allah in zaka Sani secondary kasani makaranta me kyau sbd wannan na sha wuya wallahi, Yai mata murmushi yace karki damu yar Baba insha Allah zanyi kokori in saki taji dadi sosai, umma se galla mata harara take aranta tana cewa na tsana yarinyar nan duk anbi an shagwaba yarinya,, suna zaune se Ga yayanta adam ya dawo da gudu ta tashi taje wajenshi tamai oyoyo, yace baby sister ya exams tace lafia lau, 
yau nazo ma da wani labari 
Yace Allah ko tace yes,
yace ok bari naje na Gaida su, yana shiga ya gaishe da iyayanshi sannan ya fito ya koma dakinsa nafeesa ta bishi, Yace bby sister ina jinki, tace yaya Na sama kudi, ya zare ido yace banganeba tsinta kikayi, 
tace a a Wani Saurayine babba Ya bani 
ya kara😳 zare ido yace nawa ya baki tace 200, yace ok yana tsoron Ya tambayeta akan dadilin da yasa ya bata amma besan wani fassara zatai wa maganar ba, tunda yarinya ne, yadan mata fada, ya ce daga yau kar ki kara amsan kudin Maza, bakyau cinye mutane sukeyi, Ta tsorata sosai tace bazan sakeba dama shine yace dole na Amsa, Yace Ok, a school dinku yake tace nidai ban taba ganinsaba amma idan na ganshi zan ganeshi, ya bata rai, yaji kanshi na ciwo yace ta tafi zai kwanta kuma kar ta fadawa kowa a gidan nan wani ya bata kudi,

Tace laa, na bata ma rai ko yace a a tace to menene yanzu naga ka chanza fuska yace mata bakomai, tace Ok, kowa yai shuru se abun na damunsa, chan dai ya kasa hakuri yace Kodai *Sonki yakeyi* tace yaya meye So Kuma, yace eh SO, tace nidai bansan wani Soba yai dariya yace yarinya kenaN, 

*Some months* letter, haka rayuwa taci gaba, Nafeesa ta shiga secondry School ta mata Zallah *Js2* aka sata sbd tanada kokari sosai, ta yi dan kiba kadan ta fara kirgan dangi, kyanta ya kara bayyana,  kowa a ung yana nafeesa, gata da shegen surutu yanzu dai umma ta rage mata Muguntar da take mata dan yanzu nafesa tayi hankali sosai gata da kwarjini, karamace dai amma ba wannan zai ganta be kara kallontaba dan dole ma a kalleta saboda Dirin da Allah Yai Mata....

9⃣2⃣



Washe gari tana hanyar dawowa gida ne ta hadu da kawarta maryam tace hmm feenah manya, an zama yan mata, takai mata duka tace eh din kema ai yan matan Ne, maryam tace anfa aiko nine, tace wajena kuwa tace eh, kullun inaso nazo amma ina tsoron kar umma tai fada tace in fitan mata a gida, feenah tai murmushi tace naji dai waye ya aikoki, tace yayana sagir mana yace yana *SONKI* feenah ta zare ido tace rufamun asiri kar umma taji, Feenah tai uhm tace se inkizo tajine zataji, feenah tace nidai ba ruwana wallahi, sai anjuma natafi, kicemai yayi hakuri ni yarinyace bansan meyema *SO* maryam tace zaki sani yarinya...ta kama hanya tai gida, *maryam* ma tayo gida ta tarar da Yayanta sagir tace
 yaya kasan me na fada mata, 
yace are you serious my sister 
tace yes i'm serious dear yaya 
ya ciro kudi yace thanks My sister
Ya fada gado da wani tsalle yana kallon ceiling yace yes....FEENAH INA SONKI akwai wani secret abu a mind dina da bazan bari kowa yajiba *ADALILIN SON DA NAKE MIKI BAZAN TABA BARI KI KUBCEMUN BA*...., yanzu nasan me zan fara yi......yai wani shu'umun dariya da ni karan kaina bansan me yake nufiba. Ya sauka a kan gadon zai fita ya sake wani murmushi yace yes That's me. 

Ita kuma *feenah* tana shiga gida yau da sauki ta gaishe da umma, ta amsa ta bata abinci tace tagode, ta gama cin abinci tai aikin da ta saba kullun yamma nayi ta tafi islamiya, bata hadu da yaya Adam Ba, Se Dare... Da ya dawo se ta tabbatar ya gama cin abinci sannan Taje dakin ta sameshi tace yaya wlhi na gaji bari nadan Kwanta kusa dakai...ya daka mata tsawa yace keee baki da hankaline, ko ance miki mace da namiji suna Kwanciya gado dayane ba tare da sunyi aureba, tace to kai ba yayana bane kuma ai zan iya, yai mata warning yace kar ki kuskura ina kwance ko zaune kice zaki zo kusa dani, infact ma tashi ki fitan mun a daki kar in sake ganinki, ta marairaice tace dan Allah kayi hakuri bazan sakeba wallahi, nan dai yace ok, Sukai shuru na kusan Minti Goma, Tace yaya dama tambayarka Zanyi yace inajinki.....Tace yaya Dan Allah meye *SO*❤, Kuma Ya MUtun zaiji in ana Sonshi....da sauri ya dago ya kyalleta a xuciyanshi yana cewa Whats Wrong With yarinyar nan kardai ace ta fara soyayya, Ina shirin auranta, Ta katse mai Tinani Tace yaya meye So Din please tell me, da kyar ya bude baki Yace kafin na fada miki sai kin fada mun A ina kikaji KALMAR *SO* da har zaki tambayeni, Tace A a ba kai bane rannan da nacema wani ya bani 200 ka tambayeni ko yana Sonane, Shine yau ma Sagir din gidan Alh Abdullahi ya aiko kanwarsa wai Yana Sona.... *To ni kuma bansan meye SO DIN BA INASO NE INSANI*.....TooFaa

9⃣3⃣

Ya Sunkuyar da kanshi, cos yanajin tausayin kanshine idan HAR NAFEESa Tasan MEYE SO..HE might lose her, yana tinanin Xuci ta kara ce mai Please *YAYA MEYE SO* gabanshi ya fadi amma ya daure yace oka SO Kikace ko Tace Eh...YACE _SO WANI HALITTACE A JIKIN DAN ADAM, KA KANJI SON MUTUN A DILILAI DA YAWA_ yai shuru ta tsaya tana kallonshi, tace kagamane, aranshi yace lallaima, kardai ace tasanine tazo tana tambayana, yace yes i'm done... Ta nunfasa tace Naji kace halitta ne a jikin ko wani dan Adam, nima inadashi kenan, Da kaman bazaiyi mata maganaba Yace Eh kina dashi, tace ya banganshi ba a jikina ya daga mata hannu yace it's enough, Dare ya farayi jeki daki ki kwanta gobe akwai school tace a a ai gobe weekend ne yace oh namanta, just go inaso nadan duba wani book ne kafin na kwanta, tace ok sweet dreams yaya, yace ok bby sister, tana fita ya fada kogin tinani yace Alhamdulillah, naji dadi da baki kwanta ba dan nasan irina, da ba abun da zai hana ban tabakiba, chan kuma yaja tsaki yace mtsww yarinyar nan dole na taka mata burkin shiga mun daki dan tafara girma gskiya, ni kuma nasan banda hakuri,  chan kuma tinanin sagir ya fado mai da yace yana Sonta,nan take idonshi yai ja yace *ADALILIN SON DA NAKE MIKI BAZAN TABA BARI WANI YA AUREKIBA NI YAFI CHANCHANTA NA AUREKI* toofaa,

❣❣❣❣❣❣❣❣❣❣
Shekaru sun shude, Hajiya rabi ta rasa ina zataga maimuna ta bata hakuri kullun se tazo layin ko zataga maimuna amma ina, gashi ta rasa waye zata tambaya ko yasan inda take, dan tsoro takeji ta tunkare samira dan tasan rashin mutuncin da sukai musu ranar da aka kawo beebah... Akwai wata Ran tazo Layin se jeka ka dawo take a layin ta rasa ya zatai ta shiga gidan Samira se ga _Naseer_ babban dan Alhaji kamal Ya fito daga gidan ya ganta se zirga zirga takeyi a kofar gidan, Ya gaisheta tace yauwa dama dan Allah ina tambayane ko nan ne layin gidan haj samira, yai murmushi yace gama gidan nan, ya kama hanya yai gaba, ita ko tace na shiga uku, yanzu ya zanyi in shiga dama nasan gidan kawai munafunci nane oh ni rabi, dakyar tai karfin hali ta shiga gidan da sallama da hijabinta har kasa, 
samira na ganinta ta tsuke fuska tace lafia kika shugo mun gida,
Rabi tace kiyi hakuri dan Allah nazone na tambayeki ina maimuna take yanzu,
 samira ta kara hada fuska a xuciyanta tana innalillahi wa inna ialihi rajiun, samira tace nima bansan inda takeba dan bansan meya hadasu da mijinba, kuma ki fitarmun agida yanzunan nan, wato kinzo in fada miki inda take ku bita ko, To bansaniba fitan mun a gida, kuje chan kuita abunku, tace zan fita Allah ya huce xuciyarki tunda bakisan gidan ba shikkenan Sai anjuma...tace agayas, munafukar banza da wufi, Allah yai mana tsari da irinku, Tana shiga daki ta Nema Wayanta ta kira Anty larai, take bata labarin abunda ya faru, Anty larai tace nagode da baki fada mata ba,nan dai suka jajanta ma juna Dan abun tsoro yake basu, ko ina sukaje ace sihirine Babba se anci gaba da addua.........wannan kenan

9⃣4⃣

Har Naseer yaje ya dawo Samira na dakin tai tagumi ta rasa me ke shirin faruwa da maimuna ne, Mutun yabar gidan amma se biyoshi akeyi wannan wani irin bakin kishine irin nasu, Naseer yai sallama, Ta amsa tace kadawo yace eh. Ya sama waje ya zauna, yace kinga bakuwa ko, tai maza tace mai eh, dan batason ta bashi labarin meya faru,
Ya shugo ya Zauna yake cewa mummy, naga jifa, tace ina kaje ina sha kana bakin gate ne dai, yace wallahi a a naje chan kasane siyo card, tace ok, Kaje kan dining table ga abincin ka chan kaci, 
Yace na koshi, mumy Kinsan me, 
tace a a Naseer, 
yace wallahi bansan Ganin yara marasa iyaye, tausayi suke bani, most especially naga yara almajirai suna yawo a rana, mumy Samira tace A xuciyanta Allah sarki, Nan take idonshi ya cika da kwalla mumy tace kayi hakuri, wallahi akwai tausayi, ya tashi ya busar da iska a bakinshi yace Allah ya bani wani babban mukami insha Allah marasa gata zasuji dadi.....
Ya tafi daki yadan huta, da kwanciyarshi ba sai *Feenah* Ta fado mai araiba 
yace Oh My God ina zan sake ganinki, beauty wani hali kike, Gashi Kun bar makarantar bari naje na duba...
Ya daga Hannu yace Ya Allah ka hadani da beauty, *SAI NA TEMAKETA*, wai meyasa wasu iyayan basu da tausayine, nan take tinanin marasa gata ya kamashi, harda kwallan shi, Bacci yai awon gaba dashi,....

Washe gari, Alhaji Kamal suna palour dashi da matarshi, da naseer, da zainab da zayyad, suna hira, Alhaji kamal ya kalla dansa yace to Alhamdulillahi, Tunda Ka gama service kana samun aiki aure zan maka dan haka ka fito da mata, da sauri Naseer ya kalla dad dinshi yace Dad zanyi aure amma ba yanzuba _please i'm still young ko Mumy_, Samira tai murmushi tace Alhaji su kake ka sama jika kenan, Alhaji Kamal yace a a, naga ya girmane tunda yanada hankali kamata yayi ya sama mata ya aura, samira tace eh hakane, Dana kaji abunda dadynku yace ko so please kai nemo mata Musha biki, Naseer yadan bata rai yace toh Lolz, suka dan taba hira kenan Dad yace zai tafi office, Naseer ya shiga daki yace tab, dady zai kiramun ruwa fa, aure ni a tsarina ba yanzuba Dan ko mata ban kulawa Dan banso na yaudara kowa, yaga tinanin zai dameshi ya dakko laptop dinshi yanada Dan dube dube.....

9⃣5⃣ 
Bangaren Nafeesa Kuwa kullun se maryam ta dameta akan yaya sagir, tace to naji amma dan Allah karki sake damuna ko ana So dolene, nida har yanzu banjin feeling din kowa wai da sunan *SO* Wai ya so yakene, Allah yasa wata rana naji ina son wani naji yanda akeji, I heard mutane da yawa suna So mugun abune bt ni har yanzufa bansan meye *SO* ba, kawai se na fara son mutun, tayaya zan ma fara soyayya, 
Maryam dai se kallonta take, tace ji beki awajen ahaka kamar kin waye amma ashe da sauranki, common So ne zakice bakyaji a xuciyanki, nafee tace i told you bansan ya ake Ji faba, Ko zaki iya sani naji,
 Maryam ta tsintsire da dariya harda rike Ciki, Tace wane Ni sedai yaya sagir ya koya miki ya So yake, Tace Ok maryam bari naje gida we talk tomorrow tace ok kawata, ko kicemun na gaishe da yaya sagir, tace ohh My regard duk da bansan ya so yakeba, maryam dai ta tsaya tana mamakin kawarta tace ok bye, feenah ta dawo gida, Ta tarar da Baba sani a daki yace feenah jummai batanan kuma Adam ya koma school, dan Allah dauka 100 naira dinnan kije ki Siyomun magani bayana na ciwo, Tace Allah sarki baba Sannu, bari nai sauri na dawo yace To nagode yar baba, Tana fita taci kawo da yaya sagir yasa sport wears (riga da Wando three quater me kalan blue black) yadakko sport car dinshi zai fita da yamma, Tace ina wuni yace lafia lau, ya tsaya yana kallonta yace ina Zaki tace babane ya aikeni, yace ok in bazaki damuba please 2minuite, kaman zata tafi dan sauri takeyi, Se ta tsaya tsak, 
Yace Hope kinji sakona wajen kanwata Tace eh naji amma kayi hakuri har yanzu _bansan meye kalmar So Ba, ya so yake_, 
yace *huh* a xuciyanshi shima yace wow gskiya she's beautiful ji yanda take magana amma ace batasan meye soba, yace nasan me zanyi, Yace _Feeenah_ cikin wani irin murya, tsigan jikinta ya tashi ya kara cewa _feeenah Look into my eyes_ Ta kyalleshi ido cikin ido kawai se ta tsince kanta a wani yanayi dan har jikinta na rawa dan Wani irin deep kallo sukai, yana ganin jikinta ya fara rawa yace you can go....tai maza tabar wajen, Ya bita da kallo yace yes Sai Na.........Nima banji abun da yace ba..lolz, ya shiga motanshi yasa Wakan blame it on me na *AKON* dan favorite din wakanshi kenan, har ya fara tafiya yaji bazai iyaba, Ya sama waje yai parking yana tina muryanta me dadi tace _Bansan meye SO ba, Bansan ya So yakeba_ yai murmushi yace zakisan meye *S0* With time indai kina tare dani, ya Wuce Wani Shopping Plaza Babba yai siyayyan su perfume, Kayan kwalliya, Yasai Mata waya IPHONE 6...tirkashi yace da wannan zan malleki ki insha Allah, Yai murmushi ya shiga motanshi ya dawo gida......

Ita ko bangaren ta jikinta se rawa yake taje chemist din amma ta kasa cewa komai ta sama waje tai tagumi ta zauna ta sunduma cikin kogin tinani......

9⃣6⃣

Chan taji ance ke lafiyanki kuwa, ba abu kika zo siyaba ta tashi jikinta asanyaye tace eh, yace to tinanin me kike tadanyi tsaki mtsw azuciyanta tace bansan tambaya, to in ma tinani nakeyi ina ruwanka, Ta mika kudi tace abata maganin ciwon baya na 50 naira, ya amsa yace yan mata ko tinaninshi kikeyi, tace Allah ya kyauta, yai mata dariya yace nasan shine dai zaki zauna kita tinaninshi haka lolz...ta kyalleshi tace Ikon Allah jimun mutun kaman yasan abun dake raina, Itadai ta amsa maganin ta dawo gida, baba Sani yace kin dade ina fatan lafia dai ko, Tace eh baba Layine kasan yamma yayi yace hakane, nagode yar baba Allah yayi albarka tace Amin, Ta shiga daki ta huta, dan yamma tayi taga umma bata dawoba tace uhm bari na tashi na daura abinci kafin umma ta dawo ta cinyeni da fada, Ta tashi ta daura tukunya, Ta dafa taliya yar hausa, Kafin ma tagama umma ta dawo se shi mata albarka take abun yabata mamaki tace wata rana, dare nayi tana waje ita kadai tanashan iska tunda Adam abokin hiranta benan, taji su umma suna hiran mahaifanta, sukace ikon Allah Alhaji umar shuru, Jummai tace tunda kaji shuru ai sekabi dan uwanka Kaji ya labari, baba sani yace bazanjeba, shi ina hankalinshi yake, Tunda yarinyar nan tazo gidan nan ya taba zuwa ya ganta dandai uba ubane Amma wallahi ba ban maida itaba, jummai tace Wallahi nima tausayi take bani, yarinya da gatanta amma ta zama abun tausayi, Feenah naji ta shiga daki se kuka takeyi an tsokanu mata inda yake mata ciwo dan kullun da tinanin mahaifiyarta take kwana Ta tashi, chan sukaji shasshekar kukanta da sauri suka bita dakin suna tambayanta meya faru, tace Kantane ke ciwo,
Da sauri baba yayo kanta yace kiyi hakuri sannu, kodai kinji maganar da mukeyine, Ta kara fashewa da kuka tace banjiba baba, Umma tace yo inma taji ai ba karya bane, abun fa da ciwo Malan, mutun da gatanshi da komai amma Matar uba ta hana mahaifiyarka da kai zama dan sai ta kara fashewa da kuka, Malan Sani se yi wa jummai fada yakeyi don me zata dinga sata kuka, Lallashinta ya kamata tayi bawai tai ta cewa kaza da kazaba, Dakyar dai ta samu ta tsayar da kukan dan ji take gaba daya so take taga mamanta, Ga Malan Sani besan Inda mamanta sukeba Yanzu, Ya bata hakuri yace nima abun na raina yar baba ki bari gobe zan kira Larai inji inda suke sai mu kaiki kinji ko, Nan dadi ya isheta ta kosa gari ya waye yaje taga mamanta, Washe gari nayi taje kofar gate din su Maryam tace baba tsoho maryam nanan, yace eh amma basu tashiba se zuwa anjuma, Hakanan ta hakura ta koma gida.

  *Washe gari* nayi ta tashi da ciwon maranta dakyar ta tashi ta gyara jikinta dan tanashan wahala, shuru 8,9 10am umma jummai taga bataga Feenah ba, ta nufa dakinta dan ganin meke faruwa da ita ta tarar da ita akwance tace lafia tace umma wlhi ciwon cikinane, tai mata sannu ta kawo mata abinci, 

shiko Adam se shan kamshi yakeyi wai feenah batazo ta gaisheshiba.
Shurun da yajine ya dai tashi ya leka dakin yace ke baki iya zuwa ki gaisheniba ko, dan kinfara Tsayawa da samari, ko shi ya hanakine, Ta rufe ido🙈 tace laaa Wallahi Ya Adam ba haka bane banjin dadine, yace meke damunki tace kaina ke ciwo ya ce Allah ya sauwake, Hope dai ya kawo miki magani, sedata dan ji wani iri tace haba ya Adam. Ya za ai ya kawomun magani ina gida, Adam yace ni nasan ko ciwon masoyane, ba itaba har shi seda yaji kunyar maganan, batace komai ba, yace dai Allah ya kara sauqi Tace Amee, Adam ya tafi daki xuciyanshi nacemai Why kai mata wannan maganan, ya zaka nuna kishinka karara akan wacce ko furta mata kalmar Kana *Sonta* baka tabayiba, yaji haushin kanshi ya kamashi ya tashi ya fita, 

itako feenah se juyi take A gado ta kosa Ciwom maran yadan saketa ta tashi tadan tabuka wani Abun.

I hate You, ya kara zare ido😳 me kikace tace I hate you, nan ya fara mata magiya yana bata hakuri ko laifi taji ance yayi ita shedane, Dan me zata dauka jita jitan mutane, kunsan wasu matan da saurin karaya, nan dai ya sanyar mata da jiki yace amma waya fada miki tace nidai ka bari idan munje gida mayi magana nan hanyane, dakyar ya lallabeta ta shiga Mota, yace inajinki kinga nan yafi sirri, me kikaji akaina, Tace ance kai dan iskane, Yai karfin hali irin na mazan nan yace dan iska kuma, wani irin iskanci nakeyi yana magana yana sunkuyar dakai, Tace ance kana kashewa mata kudi ka yaudaresu, ya dafa kirji yace Niiii baby love, To karya suke yi, da inayi da bakiji a unguwan muba, kawai suna miki bakin cikine, dan sunga kinada farin jini, Ki rabu dasu, Nidai nasan Inasonki, *A Dalilin Son* da nake mikine fa yasa na biyoki, kuma dani dan iskane da baance ki dinga zuwa gidanaba, haba bby love *Think*🤔....

Bangeran Maimuna kuma, abu yaqi ci yaqi cinyewa dan yanzu babanta ya damu da kyar dai anty larai ta sanar dashi abun dake faruwa, ranshi ba karamin baci yayiba, yace A kawomai yarsa, ai tanada gata, ba namijin da zai wulaqantamai yarsa, Washe gari nayi suka tafi senegal, bayan some hours suka sauka, babanta dayaganta yaci kuka kamar ranshi zai fita, Haj larai take bashi labarin tun rasuwar mamanta fa ga halin da take ciki, Baba Alhaji yai ma larai da ita maimunan fada sosai yace ba amai adalciba yar cikinsa, ai ya dace a fadamai halin da take ciki, 
anty larai dai tace kayi hakuri wallahi munasha abune da zamu iya solving ba tare da ka saniba

Yace komun kakantarshi haj larai ya kamata ki fadamun, sanin kankine ita kadaice yata, yanzu ba mamanta ita kadai zangani naji dadi, Anty larai tace hakane, ka gafarcemu Dan Allah yace na yafe amma raina ya baci, Kikace bata magana ko larai tace eh, Yace bakomai Yata karki damu insha Allah se inda karfina ya kare, Zaki warke,, Ta daga mai kai alamun Insha Allah, baba tsoho yace nagode da kikabar musu yarsu, tunda ba mutuwa yai ba suje chan su karata, kowa ya rike dansa, anty larai tace eh nima haka nai tinani, da ranshi da lafiya, dole yar abarta a dangin ubanta, yace yes hakane, nagode sosai to ke yanzu larai ya za ai, in kintafi dole na saman mata me aiki me kula da ita, larai tace Baba kabarmu mu tafi da ita ina neman mata magani sosai A chan ma, kaga nan bakasan wa zaka neman mataba kar tazo itama ta cuceta dan Allah kabarni ni tafi da ita, nifa tamkar mahaifiyanta nake, Yace eh Tabbas hakane, Nagode sosai, nasan ko bayan raina zaki rikemun yata, tamkar naki, larai tace nawane ma Alhaji, suka dan taba hira, ba karamin tausayawa maimuna yayiba, se kare mata kallo yakeyi yana cewa Ikon Allah kenan, haka matan hausawa suke da mugun kishi, Larai tace wasu ba; Ba kowa ke da irin hali nata ba gskiya, Kuma dan taga maimuna nada hakurine Sai yace yace eh bakomai, Yata insha Allah *A DALILIN* hakurin nan da kikai Zaki sama nasara, Larai tace amin insha Allah, , Gobe zamu koma muci gaba de neman mata magani dan munje wajen Wani yai mana addua sosai yace Zata warke lokaci ake jira, yace Allah yakaimu lokacin sukace Amin, Washe gari da zasu zo Baba Alhaj yai kuka dan ji yakeyi kamar karsu tafi, maimuna kadai yake gani yaji dadi aranshi, maimuna ma se kuka takeyi dan ba bakin magana, anty larai ta basu hakuri tasan Shakuwan da Da mahaifa se Allah. Da kyar yai ta maza ya lallasheta yarshi, ya basu kudin miliyan 20, Yace gashi abata abinci da duk abunda take bukata, plz, anty larai tace bazasu amsaba, sunada kudi yace yasani, sukadai amsa, sukace zasu tafi yai musu fatan alheri.....Allah Sarki, maimuna ko takaicin duniya duk yabi ya dameta taSo ta zauna wajen babanta amma ba yanda zatai, Tasan anty larai ta mata hidima sosai, koba komai itama mahaifiyartace....... "Anty larai ta kira samira a waya tace haba samira kwana biyu baki nememu ba ko lafiya,

Adam na cikin Napep se juyawa yakeyi yana Hangota  a inda ya barta a tsaye,

 Gabanshine keta dukan uku uku, Ba abun da yakeyi a cikin Napel in banda tinanin feenah har aka kawo besan Ma ankawoba...... 

Ita kuwa feenah tana shiga gidan taje ta ajiye lettern a cikin Hand bag dinta sannan ta shiga cikin yan mata sa'anta aketa Hira, 

Yan uwa se mata congrats akeyi wai she's lucky, Gata da kyau, gata da natsuwa, ga babanta me kudi,

 Ba karamin hakuri tayiba, ita dai sedai ta kyallesu tai murmushi...., 

Yan matan kuwa se iyayi sukeyi, daga wanchan saurayinta ya kirata, se wanchan ta koma gefe tana chating, 

sedai Ta kyallesu kawai tace A zUciyanta race Allah ya rabaku da Sharrin Maza mayaudara.....

 Zainab ce me dan hankalin cikinsu yarinyar Kishiyar Anty Samira da itace suketa hira akan school, 

abun gwanin sha'awa, nan zainab ke bata labarin ai mamanta neigbour din Sune idan sun dawo dan haka sai yasa feenah tafi natsuwa da ita suke hira........ 

Bayan isha'i Manyan mata na pallow yan mata kuma suna cikin bed room Se rawar kai akeyi LOLz,

           *Hello Sister  How are you doing i hope u're doing Well*

Tana gama karantawa wani waye masu zafi suka zubo mata tana cewa WHY YA ADAm me yasa baka furta mun ba..... Nima Ina sonka, 
Haka ta gama kukunta ta wanke fuska ta dawo ta kwanta kamar ba itaba Dan kar a gane halin da take ciki.......

bacci ya kwasheta....Washe  gari nayi Akai Taron maida auran *MAIMUNA DA ALHAJI UMAR*. Abun gwanin sha'awa Maimuna ba karamin farin ciki tayi sosaiba, Yan uwa da Abokan arziki kowa yai murna, Anci ansha angodewa Allah, 

Maimuna tayi kyau, Kowa yaga feenah zaka rantse ba diyar maimuna bane, dan kamar ta baci sosai, sedai ace ko kanwartace,

 Dare nayi akace za a maida mai mata, yan uwa se cece kuce akeyi akace abari zuwa gobe...

Anty larai tace aa Yau dinnan za a maidata, tunda an maida me ya rage ai kawai haka zaifi, Abunka da masu Kudi komai baja baja....

Dare nayi aka maida maimuna dakin mijinta, 

su Anty samira da kawayensu a ciki harda Hajiya rabiii tare da Diyarta feenah.....

Ba wanda ya kwana tunda ba budurwa bane.....

*Beebah* najin motsin mutane a gidan ta kwarma Ihu, Ta fito cikin gida se masifa takeyi, Ba wanda yace mata komai a ka wuce Part din Maimunah.....

Kowa yaga gidan masha Allah, 

Beebah saboda tsabar masifa Bata bari baki sun wuce ba, 

tayo part din Maimuna....Mezai faru, Suna hada ido da maimuna, maimuna ta karantu addu'oi akan Allah yai mata katangar karfe da beebah

 *beebah ta kara kwarmu Ihu....wayyo Allah na shiga uku, Dama ni nayi asiri akan Su rabu, wayyo Allah, Maimunah Na bani inji haj Beebah. Ganin ihun bana hankali bane ya maido da hankalin mutanan da ke cikin gidan, se Ihu takeyi tana tuma a kasa.....*

Nan maganar malam me rukiyya yace Abunda tayi zai koma kanta ya fado musu......

ikon Allah Haj beebah ta hau kace dan duk mugayan abubuwan da tayi yau ta fadeshi da bakinta....Dakyar dai aka samu wasu maza suka yaudareta da kida (gangar shaidan) se rawa takeyi tana  ta samu tabar Gidan.


 "Kowa se Allah ya kara yakeyi Abun mamaki, Wasu matanen se Hankali wlhi, Nan dai akai ma maimuna fada akan ta jajirce da addua sosai bazama zatai ba,

 sannan ta sa yarta addua itama, Allah yasa mu dace......

yan rakiya sun watse daga maimuna se Samira da yaransu zainab da feenah....

Suna hira ALHAJI YAI SALLAMA, ya kara godewa Samira sosai dan ba karamar kokari sukai Ba.....

Samira tace bakomai ta komaai duk temakon junane

Sannan danyi hira kadan samira ta koma gida ita da zainab. 

Se maimuna da Alhaji da feenah.....nandai ya kara musu fada suma, Akan kowanne a cikinsu ya dage da addua....sannan ya kuma basu hakuri akan abubuwan da ya faru,

*2 months LATER* haka komai yaci gaba da wakana Amma tunda feenah suka dawo bata taba zuwa gidan su Anty Samiraba, Tun tana mata korafi har ta gaji, ita ma frnd dinta zainab Ta dena zuwa tunda feenah ba zuwa takeyiba, 

Anty larai kuma tana zuwa akai akai tana duba lafiyan Yarta da jikanta feenah:....   

 *FEENAH* tunda Suka dawo kullun bata wani walwala in kunga tayi to dan uwanta Adam nanan ne, Tun mumy na tambayanta meka faruwa se tace ita Ba komai dan ta rasa yanda zatai ta fadawa mamanta SHE IS NOT VIRGIN...      

"Bangaren Sags kuma kullun cikin mafarkin feenah Yake, dan tun bayan tafiyanta ya tuba, Ya zama ustaz din dole, Sedai ya kulle kanshi a daki yaita kuka yana rokan Allah ya yafemai dan ba karamar cutar da feenah yayiba,

 Yayi neman Amma har ya gaji, Gashi be ganin ADAM  a unguwansu shine ya kara daga mai hankali yana cewa kodai Aure Sukayine, Yaita tinani barkatai abun tausayi,         

 Alhaji umar ne zaune a daki suka tattauna da matarshi akan Shifa Yarsu Makaranta zata koma ba zaman gida zatai ta yiba,

 Maimuna ta bada goyon baya dari bisa Dari tace nima Abunda ke Raina kenan tunda miji bai fitoba se ta koma School,

 Aiko washe gari nayi tunda lokacin daukan New student ne akaje akai mata rejistration *A KADUNA STATE UNIVERSITY*, sbd Dad dinta yace ba yanzu zatai aureba karatu zatayi sosai, Kasancewar ita dayace a wajensu,  taso tayi aure ita da wuri amma bata samu tayiba,  

Feenah tace ita Ba karatun da zatayi, se tirje kafa takeyi akan ita Sam Boko ya isheta Aure za ai mata Adam ne ya kirata ya bata hakuri akan cewa tabi umarnin iyayanta zatajii dadi, ta nuna Yanzu wasu suna chan suna neman wa zai temakesu su samu su shiga makaranta yadai mata nasiha Karshe dai ta amince Tace ta gode yace yawa baby Sister.....

Sukace Okay,  
*SORRY READERS a GURGUJE* Da fitarshi Adam ne ya tsugunna ta yace Dan Allah ku saurareni zanyi magana, Sukace munaji, 

Nan ya fara ce musu suyi hakuri ko wani bawa da abun dake samunsa arayuwa, Gaban dai mumy se dukan uku uku yakeyi, Nan ADam ya basu labarin abun daya faru da feenah From *A To Z* amma suyi hakuri, basu sanar dasu tun daga farkoba,

 Dakyar Adam da Samira suka lallasheshu akan sui shuru yanzu idan Alhaji yaji kumafa, 

ayi shuru a rufa mata asiri dan Allah,   

 Nan maimuna kuma ta danyi tinani tace hakane kuma, duk lafinmune, da muka barki, dan duk mace tana bukatar Uwa a kusa,

 Anty larai ma Tadanyi jum tace eh hakane kiyi Hakuri feenah, Allah ya baki lafiya,    itadai feenah na

 kwance ga ciwon kai ga tsoron abun da zata tashi Ai mata duk taji dama ta mutu....

 Anan dai suka samu suka fito waje, aka bar feenah da zainab, zainab dai ba karamin tausayi tabata, tana bata hakuri feenah se kuka takeyi,    Se anty larai ne suka dan tattauna akan kar abari alhaji Ya ji dan Allah, zasusan yanda za ai,   


  kwanan feenah 2 aka sallamesu suka dawo gida,   

    Nan maimuna ta fara nuna mata So da kauna, tana mata nasiha kuma tai mata fada sosai akan a kiyaye gaba, 

Anty samira ma tazo ta kara mata fada akan aci gaba dai da hankali da maza...  

  Feenah Tace *Alhamdulillah* Dama abun dake damunta kenan a rayuwarta,

 yanzu bata da wani damuwa bata da matsala,  zata ci gaba da rayuwarta cikin Kwanciyar hankali,   ta tashi taje tai wa Adam godiya sosai,     

      2days shuru shuru ba Feenah a school dan haka ummi yau takirata take tambayanta yayane, nan 
feenah ke bata labarin abubuwan daya faru Ummi tace wow Masha Allah.... Naji dadi nima.... 

Yanzu ba wata damuwa ko feenah,

 Feenah tace eh Alhamdlh, gobema insha Allah zanzo school sukai sallama,

nan dai suka danyi hira kadan,   ummi ta kyalla feenah tace har wani kyau kikayi, feenah tace kai Ummi banaso fa, haka dai suka karata suka Gama hiransu har akai closing        


feenah ta dawo gida around 4pm taji wayanta na ringing, ta duba taga Maryam 

Feenah tai tsalle tace kawata ya kike ya kwana biyu, 

"maryam tace lafiya lau se alkhairi,

     "maryam kuma suna gama wayan taje ta sanar da sags tace ta kirata tace Tananan Allah ya kaimu,

           Washe gari nayi feenah ta tashi da wuri tai abincin kala biyu, ta hada drinks, sannan tai kwalliya tana jiran frnd dinta maryam, 


har zainab ta kira akan suzo su tarbata kawarta,   

 Su kuma su sags already sun kama hanya, 

"Suna hanyane maryam ta kira feenah akan suna hanya....  


  Feenah tace Ohk.                   Basu dde ba suka iso Kaduna garin gwamna,

 Maryam tace gatanan   feenah tace ki shugo me gadi zai bude miki gate, 

maryam ta shugo, da gudu feenah da zainab sukazo tarbeta sukai ciki, 

Se murna feenah takeyi, suka rakata sukaje gaishe da Mumy da Adam, Yai tsaki yace Allah ya kyauta, 


Nan dai suka shiga ciki su Uku se faman hira sukeyi kamar parrots, chan anjuma Maryam tace suzo waje sudan gaisa da wanda ya kawota,  


 Zainab tace dama bake kaadai bane,     maryam tace Eh...

 Suka wuce suka Tafi, Suna fita sags ya fito a motan da sauri kamar zai chafke Feenah,


 Gabanta ne ya fadi yace dum! 

Sags Yace nazo baki hakurine wallahi ba komai nake nema wajenkiba, 

Hawaye suka zubuwa feenah ta kasa cewa koma, 
ita dai zainab tana ganinshi tagane shine yai mata aika aika,


 maryam dai se kara bata hakuri takeyi, Dakyar dai feenah tace ta hakura, 

Sannan sags ya kuma cikin motan, 

Ba feenah ba har zainab seda sukaji tausayinsa ya kamasu,

 Zainab tace Allah sarki wallahi da alama ya tuba, hope dai kin yafemai har zuciyanki,

 feenah tace na yafee, sannan suka shiga gida, Feenah tace dan Allah zainab karki bari kowa Yasan sags ne yazo dan Allah, 

  zainab tace haba sist wlhi ba wanda zaiji, nan dai suka kara kwantar wa feenah da hankali, 

maryam tace zata tafi nan mumy ta hada mata shatara ta arziki, suka rakota har bakin gate, 

sukai sallama, su sags suka kama hanya sai gida, Sannan zainab itama ta kara bata hakuri akan yanzu ai komai ya wuce, munawa Allah laifi yana hakura balle kuma mu..... 


Feenah tace hakane, Sannan sukai Sallama, zainab ta koma gida,


         *feenah* ce ta dakko wayanta tai ma sags Text kamar Haka,    _Hello how ar you doing, Allah ya kaiku gida lafiya, thanks for the visit, Kuma inaso na fadamane Na yafema Allah ya kara yafe mana gaba daya,  sannan kuma plz karka kara zuwa gidan mu, this will be the 1st and the last, kar ka sani cikin wahala_ Tai sending,   


Sags ne yana driving yaje wayanshi yai kara alamun text ya shugo, Ya dauka ya karanta,

 bt unfornunate bazai iya yin reply,

Yana gama karantawa Yaji kanshi yayi dum... Bt beyi reply ba, yaci gaba da driving,   

ita kuma feenah ta rike waya gam ahannu tana jiran Taga reply shuru, shuru ba reply itama,  Ta sake mai text _baka text dina bane ko_,    

yasake karantawa bece mata komai ba... 

  Feenah taga text ya tafi ba reply ta fara wani kumbure kumbure wai wannan ai rainine,             

 ba sags yai reply ba se da suka koma gida ya huta sannan ya dakko ya sake karantawa nan yai mata reply kamar haka....   

 _Fatan alheri a gareki, dazu naga text dinki ina driving bt tunda kince haka ba komai... Zan ma chanza sim insha Allah bazan sake kiraba, Thanks for the good time we had when together, na fita hakkinki na baki hakuri bye.. Sai wata Rana Allah Ya kara tsareki, Yana sending ya kashe wayanshi shima da niyyar gobe zaije ya chanza sim_ 

too faa feenah na gama karantawa tace to ina ruwana dayafi maka......


Se washe gari ta dakko wayanta ko zaataga text ya bata hakuri by shuru, 

Ta zauna tana tinanin texts din data mai be kamataba, Shine ta fara dialing numbern sags shuru shuru, be shiga, 
taji kuma wani sonshi na shigaNta Azuciyanshi tana cewa Allah yasa dai ba chanza sim din da gaske yayi ba, 

Haka tai ta kira shuru, har ta kira maryam akan meyafaru da wayan Sags, Tace naji kamar yana cewa zai saida wani abune?  

   Feenah tace are you serious, 
Maryam tace wallahi da gaske nakeyi ko inmai maganane   

 feenah Tace No. Shikkenan! 

Suna gama waya feenah ta tusa kanta a pillow tana kuka tana Allah yasa ba ba saida wayan yayiba.

naseer ya dawo gida ya kira *L M D C T* sunan da yai saving kenan da numbern feenah , Kira daya biyu har 3 batai picking ba yaji gabanshi ya fadi, yace lafiya Kuwa!! 

  Chan yaji mumy samira na cewa Ina *naseer* din plz kazo Nafeesa batada lafiya suna asibiti, 

Da sauri Nas ya fito yace ku shiga wani asibitin Kenan aka kaita, mumy samira ta kira Maimuna sukace suna asibitin *GIWA HOSPITAL* da sauri ya kama hanya basui 30mins ba suka isa,

 suka shiga suka sameta a kwance, tana bacci mumy samira tace meke damuntane haka, 

Ganin Naseer ne da Adam a dakin Yasa Maimuna tace har yanzu basu fadaba, 

 ankira sallan azahar ne Adam da Naseer suka gaisa Suka fito waje dan yin Sallah..

Adam da naseer dukda suna ganin juna a waje, a layinsu but dukansu Seda sukaji haushin juna kowa na sakawa axuciyanshi yana cewa *KODAI SAURAYINTANE*, 

se kallon kallo akeyi bt kowa na ciki na ciki.........

*Washe Gari!!!* Friday Bayan sallah jumma'a Alhaji kamal Ya dawo daga abuja, Ya fita sallan la'asar kenan Suka hadu da Alhaji Umar shima ya dawo daga wajen aikinshi, Nan dai suka gaisa sukacewa junansu basu da kirki ga gida ga gida amma ba wanda ya damu adinga zumunci,

Alhaji Umar yai dariya yace Alhaji Kamal kenan Ai yanzu alhamdlh komai na tafiyamun dai dai,

 Alhaji Kamal yace masha Allah ai haka akeso Nan dai suka dan tattauna akan su dinga zumunci,

 ALHAJI kamal yace Ai matanmu sunfimu zumunci gaskiya,

 Alhaji Umar yace eh gaskiya To inanan zuwa anjuma agidanka zan ci dinner insha Allah, nan sukai sallama kowa ya kama harkan gabanshi,

   Alhaji kamal ne bayan ya dawo daga masallaci yakecewa matarshi ai yanzu ya hadu da Alhaji Umar yananan ma zuwa gidan nan anjuma wai anan zaiyi dinner, Samira tace ok badamuwa Allah ya kaimu anjuma, Alhaji yace ameen, Sai ki san abun da zaki girka mana!
 Samira tai dariya dan tasan indai fannin abincine bata da damuwa!!

 Nan Alhaji Kamal Yace dama inasone a hada auran Naseer da yar gidan kawarki Maimuna wato Nafeesah!! 

*SAMIRA* tace to Amma dai ku tsaya mugani wazai dakko, dan jiyan nan mukai magana dashi Akan cewa ya samo mata, dama ina jirane ka dawo mui maganar aje gidan iyayanta a nema izini,  

Alhaji Kamal yace au au au hakane, Gaskiya bamu Zauna ba tunda nadawo to shikkenan Bari mugani kafin mu yanke namu hukuncin,

 Alhaji Umar yace ah to shikkenan na, Ai mu duk iyayanshine kowaye ya dakko indai ta mana Alhamdlh....

 Nan samira ta tashi ta barsu su Alhaji sukaci gaba da maganarsu!


 Ba Alhaji Umar yabar gidan ba se 10Pm, Alhaji Kamal ne ya rako Alhaji umar har bakin Gate sannan sukai Sallama!  


     Alhaji Umar ne ya shiga gida yaga duk sun kwanta, ya shiga dakin matarshi har ta fara bacci ya tasheta,

 Maimuna taji haushi sosai dan a tinaninta alhaji neman Aure yakeyi dan be sanar da itaba ga inda zaije,

 Alhaji Umar ne yaga fuskar matanshi duk a daure yake tambaya me ya faru, 

tace mai bakomai ina kaje Haka, 

Nan Alhaji umar ke bata labarin ai gidan Alhaji Kamal yaje sai yasa be dawo da wuriba ya bata labarin duk yanda sukai har alhaji kamal nacewa kodai za a hada auran feenah da naseer ne Samira tace yace ya samo mata mujira muga zabinshi in beyiba se a chanza,


 Sannan maimuna ta sakko daga fushin da take, Tace to Allah dai ya ba kowa me alheri, *Nima nace wanda suke neman aure yan mata da zaurawa Ya Allah ka hadau da mazaje nagari* Ameen ya Allah


Sannan yace ba bacci zasui ba, su tashi suje pallow yanaso ya kalla wani news a bbc, hakanan ta tashi taje ta dakko mai abinci yace ai yaci gidan Su Alhaji Kamal 

tace eh nasan zakaci ai wannan na matarkane kaci ka samun Albarka, 

Alhaji umar yaci yace wow yai Dadi gaksiya, Allah dai ya saka da alheri matata, 


Tace ameen Thanks mijina.........!

Haka rayuwa tace gaba Tunda akai auran suna zamansu lfiya ba abunda ya taba hadasu, Naseer ko irin anzo ganin amaryan nan yan unguwa be bari sedai yace Tana school,

*2 months* Letter Yau Saturday feenah ce ta tashi da kasala sosai a jikinta bata iya tabukai komai se Amai takeyi jikinta dai yana mata wani iri badai yanda ta saba jiba! Dan haka ko tashi ta daura musu break fast batai Ba. Gashi NAS zai fita da wuri, Yajita dai shuru a daki ya leko yaganta a kwance duk daukanshi bacci takeyi dan haka yaje mai mata peck a goshi yace I love you ni na fita, sannan ya sa Kai ya fita, 

ita tananan kwance tanajin motsinshi amma ba yanda zatai ne,


NAS ya Fita office yaji dai gabanshi na fadi like something is wrong a wani waje dan haka yake ji indai wani abu yana faruwa da wani nashi Zai dingaji gabanshi na mugun Fadi dan haka yaketa karanta Hasbunallahu wani'eemal Wakeel, 

  Feenah ce kwance a daki se Amai takeyi a cikin daki kamar hanjinta zai fito common waya ba bazata iya dauka ta kira wani ba balle asan halin da take ciki!!

 Yana parking da azamarshi ya shiga cikin pallow tun a pallow yaji ana nishi wayyo Allah, Wayyo Zan mutu, 

Da sauri NAS Ya shiga dakin da gudu ya ganta cikin Amai tana yunkuri kamar Zata fado daga gado, Wani Zaranannar Ihu Nas ya sake da ya kara Tsoratar da feenah dan Batasan ma Wani Ya shugo Ba, 

" cikin Azama ya dauketa Se cikin Mota ya nufa *GIWA HOSPITAL* suna Zuwa aka kaita Emergency room akai mata gwaje gwaje nan take akasa mata drip Dan ta wahala sosai,

"Zainab na shiga dakin taga Amai duk a tsakar daki tace subhanallah me ya faru haka, shi Ya naseer din yana ina har haka ta faru, Taga tinani ba shine abunyi ba ta fara gyra dakin ta dakko tsumma ta kwashe sannan ta cire bedsheet din ta jika da ruwan omo, sannan ta dakko Mopper da ruwa ta fara gyara cikin Dakin!   

Ita kuma anty  sameera Tana shiga Aka nuna mata dakin Da aka kai feenah ta shiga da sauri ta sameta da drip a hannununta, Take tambayar naseer da ya dukar dakai alamaun yana cikin tsananin Damuwa, Anan yake bata labarin yadawo daga Office kenan ya sameta cikin Amai,

 Anty Sameera tace Ya salam Amma dai da sauki dai ko Yace yes Alhamdlh, Tace karya damu, Bari ta kira maman feenah din a sanar dasu Kar mui abun mu Kadai dan haka ta dakko waya ta kirata ba a ddeba sega Anty Maimuna tazo ta samesu suka gaisa, tace meke damunta haka, Nan naseer yace wlhi DOCTOR be fadaba tukunna, bt kamar typhoid ne dan naga harda jiri tace tanaji, 

Nan dai suka ce Allah ya kara sauki, sukace Ameen, 

Suna cikin Hiransu Sega Zainab tazo itama ta shugo da Sallama sannan tai ma feenah ya jiki tace da sauki, sun jima kadan Anty Samira tace zata koma gida ita Zainab din ta zauna tare da feenah zata dawo anjuma da Alhaji idan ya dawo, 

 Maimuna ma tace muje mu hada hanya, Allah dai ya bata lafiya sukace Ameen, 

Duk NaS yabi ya basu tausayi.           


Zainab ne a dakin Nas yaje yin Sallah feenah ta farka da alama abinci take bukata, Ta bude ido taganta a asibiti, but batace komai dan Tasan ta sha wahala sosai, Nan feenah ke tambayan Zainab ina Nas, zainab tace yaje sallah amma yanzu zai dawo bari na sako miki abinci kici kinji, sannu, 

Feenah kawai tace uhm Ok Thanks,

 Zainab na cikin zubawane sega Naseer ya dawo yace yauwa sister nagode, Ya kalle matarshi abun sonshi abun alfari, yace mata sannu yatakeji yanzu, Tai nuni da hannunta ta taba cikinta tace yunwa takeji, 

Naseer yaji dadin haka dan dama Beso tamai gaddama akan bazataci abinci ba, dan Haka Ya amsa food din da Zeee ta saka ya fara feeding din feenah, taci sosai sannan tace ta koshi,

 Naseer yaji dadi yake tambayanta yanzu meye ke mata ciwo tace ita ba komai Kawai batajin karfin jikintane! 

Haka dai suka ci gaba kwanansu biyu asibiti Aka sallemeta,

1⃣5⃣8⃣
*AGURGUJE* haka dai rayuwa taci gaba ko wani bangere,

 *4months letter* Zainab ce ta zo gidan  feenah ta sameta kwance Daki biji Bini,

  Zainab ta shugo tace haba dear kamar bakene a gidan ba, Kodai ansamune??? 

 Feenah tace uhm wlhi tun Safe yayanki yai tafiya Asma'u kuma ta tafi school so banjin dadin jikinane, hatta abinci ma na kasa daurawa!

 zainab dai tai kitchen cikin 10 mins ta dafa mata indomie ta kawo mata, Dakyar feenah ta tashi taci, tace kai thanks sister wlhi har naji wani karfi! Bari na gyara dakin ma kawai!

  Zainab tai dariya tace oh wato ma dai yunwa ne kikeji shine zaki wahalar mana da baby ko? 

"Feenah tace uhm Ba wani baby fa, sukadai tashi suna gyara gidan Zainab na lura da yanayinta tana yin abu sanyi sanyi kamar wata me Ciki, Ko ince ma me ciki! 

Bayan sun gama ne feenah ta shiga wanka, Ta sako wata doguwar riga sannan suka zauna suna hira,

 zainab dai ta kara lura tabbas dai feenah dauke take da ciki, feenah ce tace plz Xeetah yaushe zaki aure, kinga dai kin girma fa inaso ki aure!

  Zainab tace sha kuruminki kwanan nan ZanYi aure insha Allah 

  Feenah tace wa zaki aura,  zainab tace ya Adam, 

feenah ta zare ido tace amma da gske kike,
 Zainab tace wlhi i'm serious, 
Ba bacin rai ko bakin ciki feenah ta rungumeta tace Kincika mun burina wlhi abun da naso kenan, naso na fada miki ku daidaita da ya Adam se nai tinanin ko bazakiso shiba, 

Zainab itama seda tai mamakin maganar daya fito a bakin feenah dan tasan sunason junansu!

 Haka dai feenah tai mata congrats tace Allah ya kaimu ranar sukace ameen. 

    Washe gari nayi Bayan Naseer ya dawo feenah ce cikin dare tana amai hankalin Naseer ya tashi da sauri ya kaita asibiti cikin daren nan akai mata gwaje gwaje aka gane ciki take dashi na 5 months, 

Naseer yace ciki
 doctor yace yap, 

baka yanayintaba, cikin yabi jikintane,

 Naseer yai mamaki sosai, suna cikin dakine naseer ya shafa cikin yai adduoi sosai Akan Allah ya tsareta da abun da ke cikin cikinta! 


Safiya nayi suka wuce gidan iyanshi dan zai tafi office


Anan yake sanar dasu cewa feenah ciki gareta sunyi murna sosai kamar su cinyeta dan har Alhaji Kamal shima har kukan murna yayi yake cewa Allah ya bashi tsawon rai yaga jikinshi!!   

Misalin karfe 4 ne na yamma Anty samira da mumy maimuna suna tare a daki suna hira, FEENAH ta bude baki tace Ashe mun kusa shan biki,

 maimuna da samira har suna hada baki suna cewa bikin wa Kuma? 

Feenah tace Tace na Adam da Zainab mana! 

Kowa ya kalleta akan basu gane maganar da takeyiba, 

Nan ta kwashe labari tsab ta basu tace zainab ta fada mata da bakinta! Maimuna tai salati tace ikon Allah wato yaran nan soyayya sukeyi yo mu ina muka sani,

 MAIMUNA Ta kira zainab ta tambayeta nan zainab ta fada musu eh soyayya sukeyi, 

Feenah tace kunjiko, Ana hakane sega naseer ya shugo nan shima yaji labari yace masha Allah, Allah ya kara hada kansu mudai yan bikine, 

sun danyi magana kadan yace zasu koma gida, feenah ta tashi ta shirya suka kama hanya.....    

 Da isarsu gida Naseer se wani jijji da feenah yakeyi mosti daya tayi se ya ce tana damun babynsu!    




Bangaren su Alhaji kamal kuwa sameera ta fesa mai ai zainab da Adam soyayya sukeyi, ya kirawo zainab yaji ta bakinta, sannan ya nema Alhaji Umar yace yarannan ashe soyayya sukeyi

 Alhaji Umar yace kabar yara kawai!! 

 Nan dai abun yai musu dadi matuka baban Adam ma yaji dadi sosai domin kuwa yafi kowa murna wai yau shima  dansa gashi zaiyi aure!


 Manya sukai magana akasa biki wata *4* Adam da zainab se soyayya sukeyi dan yanzu tunda aka Sani har gida yake zuwa tadi tunda ansanshi!  

    Akwana atashi ba wuya Feenah ta shiga watan haihuwarta gashi watan bikin su Adam!          

An shiga satin bikin kenan yan uwa sun fara taruwa feenah taso tun ana saura 1 week bikin tazo amma ina tana chan *tana fama da kanta* tama manta da wani  biki, 

Hankalin Adam a tashe ya dakko matarshi ya kawo asibiti, Tana cikin labour room tana shan wahala Nasser duk hankali a tashe bata dde sosai ba ta haifa yarta *BOUNCING BABY GIRL*: Naseer tunda ya rugume yarshi kamar bazai saketaba,  

 ya kira gida yace feenah ta haihuwa kowa se murna yakeyi, Ranar da ta  aka dawo da ita gida! 

Bayan sati daya ranar daurin auran *Adam da zainab* akai suna, Abu ya hadu biyu ga Suna ga biki, 

Ranar suna yarinya Taci suna *ZAINAB* Amarya zainab taji dadi sosai ansa sunanta! 

Adam bma yaji dadi sosai da feenah ta sa Sunan matarshi!!!


   Haka dai aka kai amarya, yan biki da yan suna kowa abun yamai dadi dan sun jefa tsuntsu biyu da dotse daya.... 

Ranar da aka kai zainab *Adam* ba karamin shagali sukai ba dan burinshi ya ciki shima ya jishi da mata.... 

Haka komai ke wakana yanzu Adam da naseer bame jin kansu dan Adam ya sama promotion sosai yanzu haka wajen aikinsu daya da Naseer........  

Zainab tayi girma sosai naseer kullun yana makele da babynshi a hannu....
